[Director 指令] ## 批准进入 Step 5 (Iterative Refinement)

**立即开始。** R 还在卡模型下载，没有明确 ETA。D085 framing 下的稿件质量已足够提交 Reviewer 审查。

**执行策略**：
1. **先编译确认**：`paper_compile_and_check` 确认能编译 + 页数 ≤ 8 页。如果超页先压缩再提交 Reviewer。
2. **提交 Reviewer 审查**：按你的 Step 5 流程走。
3. **异步 Patch**：R/F/H 结果到了随时 Patch 补入，不阻塞 review 循环。Process-κ 跨模型数据同理。
4. **引用验证**：14 篇新增论文 `verified: false`，在 Step 5 review 期间或之后做 reference audit。

**如果 R 在 review 期间出结果**：
- R 成功 → 暂停 review，做 V2 reframe（intro/abstract/discussion/conclusion + 新 §5.6），然后重新提交 Reviewer
- R 失败 → 继续当前 D085 review 循环，R 失败本身作为额外证据 Patch 进 appendix

开始。

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01E3zjDdyLB6boDrEFESjpUa`</summary>

````text
1	---
2	name: paper-refinement
3	description: Step 5 论文迭代修改循环——Reviewer 评分循环 + 收敛判断 + paper_checklist 注入，max 5 轮。Paper Agent 进入 Step 5 时 Read 本 skill。
4	scope: global
5	tags:
6	  - workflow
7	  - research
8	  - writing
9	  - academic
10	  - review
11	version: 1
12	roles:
13	  - paper_agent
14	---
15	
16	# Step 5 — Iterative Refinement
17	
18	Step 4 全部章节落盘后，Paper Agent 进入 Step 5：把全文递交 Reviewer 评分，按反馈修改，直到收敛。
19	
20	**核心约束**：
21	- 串行——一轮 Reviewer 返回结果处理完才进下一轮，禁止并发请求
22	- 评分驱动——overall 提升才 accept 本轮，下降则 git revert
23	- 上限 5 轮——硬约束，达到上限即停，不论分数
24	
25	## 状态记录（git 是 truth source）
26	
27	每轮修改前 commit 当前论文目录，作为 revert 锚点。无需额外 state 文件。
28	
29	```
30	git -C projects/{project} add paper/
31	git -C projects/{project} commit -m "[paper] refinement round {N-1}"
32	```
33	
34	Round N 的 commit message 用 `N-1`，即 round 1 修改前 commit `round 0`（Step 4 落盘后的初稿）。
35	
36	## 单轮流程
37	
38	```
39	Round N（N = 1..5）：
40	
41	1. git commit 当前版本（message: "[paper] refinement round N-1"）
42	   └── 落地评分下降时的 revert 锚点
43	
44	2. 组装审查请求：
45	   - read_project_file("paper_checklist.yaml") — 用户自定义必查项
46	   - 拼 LaTeX 全文（或 Patch 模式下的差异）+ writing_plan.yaml 路径
47	   - 末尾追加 "## 用户自定义必查项"（见下方"checklist 注入格式"）
48	
49	3. paper_agent_request_review(text=组装后的请求, project=...)
50	   └── 请求末尾追加维度提醒："除标准的 Originality/Clarity/Soundness/Significance/Presentation 维度外，请额外检查：叙事一致性（各章节是否讲同一个故事）、贡献-范围对齐（Intro contribution ↔ 正文 section 1:1）、去 AI 风格（em-dash、-ing、filler words）。参考 reviewer-audit-checklist §7。"
51	   └── Reviewer 通过 reviewer_post_to_director 回传评分
52	
53	4. 解析 Reviewer 响应：
54	   - 维度分（Originality/Clarity/Soundness/Significance/Presentation，1-4）
55	   - overall（1-10）
56	   - 各条具体问题 severity（critical/warning/info）
57	   - checklist 项 PASS/FAIL
58	   ★ 任一 checklist FAIL → 该条问题 severity 自动按 critical 对待
59	
60	5. 按问题类型分流处理：
61	   - 事实性错误（数字不一致 / 引用错位）→ 自己用 edit_paper_section 改
62	   - 段落级叙事问题（逻辑断裂 / 强度词超限）→ spawn Worker 重写段落，回来再 edit_paper_section
63	   - 结构问题（章节顺序 / 段落归并）→ 自己用 edit_paper_section
64	   - critical 且 claim 本身无支撑 → 汇报 Director（不要自行降 claim）
65	
66	6. 全部问题处理完后判断本轮 overall 走势：
67	   - overall(N) ≥ overall(N-1) → 保留本轮修改，进入收敛判断
68	   - overall(N) <  overall(N-1) → git checkout 上一轮 commit，回退本轮所有修改，标记"本轮无进展"
69	
70	7. 收敛判断（任一满足即停）：
71	   - 全部问题为 info（无 critical / warning）
72	   - N == 5（硬上限）
73	   - 连续两轮 overall 持平（差 ≤0.1）
74	   - 连续两轮无新增 critical（critical 列表内容相同或更短）
75	
76	   未收敛且 N<5 → 进入 Round N+1
77	   已收敛 → 进入收尾（见下文）
78	```
79	
80	## checklist 注入格式
81	
82	`read_project_file("paper_checklist.yaml")` 拿到 items 后，在审查请求末尾追加：
83	
84	```
85	## 用户自定义必查项
86	以下是用户指定的必须检查的项，每项必须明确标注 PASS 或 FAIL：
87	- [chk_001] {text}
88	- [chk_002] {text}
89	...
90	
91	如果任何一项标注为 FAIL，整体 severity 自动提升为 critical（必须打回修改）。
92	```
93	
94	- checklist 为空 / 文件不存在 → 不追加该段，正常请求
95	- 不要重写 checklist 文本——保持用户原话，让 Reviewer 自己定位
96	- Dream 模式禁止注入（Dream 论文不投，格式检查无意义）
97	
98	## 修改 Worker 任务模板
99	
100	段落级修改派 Worker（不是整章重写）时：
101	
102	```
103	spawn_worker(
104	  task_type="paper_writing",
105	  project=...,
106	  task_prompt="""
107	Reviewer 在 {section} 章发现以下问题，需要你修改对应段落：
108	
109	【问题位置】{paragraph_id / 行号}
110	【问题描述】{Reviewer 原文}
111	【建议方向】{Reviewer 给的方向}
112	
113	当前段落原文：
114	```latex
115	{原文片段}
116	```
117	
118	参考资料：
119	- 写作规范：Read skills/global/latex-paper.skill.md + red-section.skill.md
120	- registry 切片：{相关 exp_id 列表}
121	- 同章节已有内容：{section}.tex 整章
122	- 引用：references.bib（只用已 verified 条目，未验证标 [UNVERIFIED]）
123	
124	要求：
125	- 只改问题段落，不动其他内容
126	- 输出修改后的段落 latex（标记 old / new）
127	- 不要画图、不要补实验
128	- 改完即返回，不写额外说明
129	"""
130	)
131	```
132	
133	Worker 返回后 Paper Agent 用 `edit_paper_section(project, section, old_text=..., new_text=..., claims=..., table_cells=..., figures=...)` 落盘——`edit_paper_section` 自动同步 provenance，不会 deprecate 未触及的 claim。
134	
135	## 收敛后收尾（Step 5e）
136	
137	Reviewer 评分循环收敛后**必须执行完整三件套**——视觉 + 引用 + 溯源**全部跑完**再汇报 Director；不允许只跑一两项就提交。任一发现 critical/blocker 都要在汇报中显式列出，不得"建议提交审稿"压过失败信号。
138	
139	### 三件套执行顺序
140	
141	按下面顺序串行（每一步完成才进下一步，前一步失败不绕过）：
142	
143	```
144	1. paper-visual-review 排版检查
145	   └── Read skills/global/paper-visual-review.skill.md
146	   └── pdf_to_images(pdf_path=projects/{project}/paper/main.pdf, pages="1-N", dpi=200)
147	       逐页检查图表分辨率 / 字号 / 边距溢出 / 公式 / 引用编号 / 缺失引用占位 [?]
148	   └── cleanup_pdf_images(output_dir=<上一步返回的 output_dir>)  # 不留临时文件
149	   └── 收集 findings：[{page, severity, issue}]
150	   └── 出现 critical（图表溢出 / 缺失编号 / 公式乱码）→ 记入 visual_report，**不**立即返工，
151	       与 verify_references / check_provenance_completeness 的结果**一并汇报 Director**
152	
153	2. verify_references 最终引用验证
154	   └── verify_references(paper_dir="projects/{project}/paper", project=..., skip_online=False)
155	   └── 返回字段：status (ok/orphan_bib/missing_bib/not_found/mismatch) 按条聚合
156	   └── critical 阈值：
157	        - missing_bib（正文 \cite 但 .bib 缺）→ 必须修，否则 LaTeX 编译失败
158	        - not_found（OpenAlex 找不到）→ 疑似幻觉引用，自己 WebSearch 二次验证；仍找不到 → critical
159	        - mismatch（年份/作者偏差）→ warning
160	   └── 自己能改的小问题（missing_bib、年份） → edit_paper_section 修；改完**再调一次 verify_references** 确认通过
161	
162	3. check_provenance_completeness 溯源覆盖
163	   └── check_provenance_completeness(project=...)
164	   └── 返回：tier_distribution / untracked_numbers / invalidated_claims
165	   └── 关键阈值：
166	        - Tier 3 占比 > 30% → 必须汇报 Director（红线，不得静默交付）
167	        - Tier X（invalidated，实验失败/被作废）≥ 1 条 → critical：claim 失去支撑，汇报 Director 决策是否改 claim
168	        - untracked_numbers（正文出现但 provenance 没追踪的数字）≥ 5 → warning：派 Worker 补 provenance
169	```
170	
171	### 汇报 Director 模板
172	
173	三件套全部跑完后**统一**汇报（不要分三次发），把所有 critical/warning 列在一处便于 Director 决策：
174	
175	```
176	paper_agent_post_to_director(
177	  text="[写作进展] Step 5 收尾完成
178	         迭代轮数：{N}/5
179	         最终 overall：{score}
180	         checklist：全部 PASS / 还有 X 条 FAIL（已修复 / 待 Director 决策）
181	
182	         ===== 视觉检查 =====
183	         检查页数：{n}/{total}
184	         结论：PASS / 发现 X 条 critical + Y 条 warning
185	         {若有 critical：逐条列出 page + 描述}
186	
187	         ===== 引用验证 =====
188	         总数 X 条，已验证 Y 条
189	         critical：missing_bib X 条 + 疑似幻觉 Y 条（已尝试 WebSearch 二次验证 → 结果）
190	         warning：mismatch X 条
191	         {若有 critical：列出条目 + 已采取的修复}
192	
193	         ===== Provenance 覆盖 =====
194	         Tier 分布：T1 {a}% / T2 {b}% / T3 {c}% / TX {d}%
195	         {若 T3 > 30% 或 TX ≥ 1：列出受影响的 claim/figure/table}
196	         未追踪数字：X 个（详见 untracked_numbers）
197	
198	         ===== 建议 =====
199	         {以下任选其一}
200	         A. 视觉/引用/provenance 全部 PASS → 建议提交审稿（submit_paper_to_review）
201	         B. 存在 critical → 请 Director 决策：{具体问题列表 + 建议修复方向}
202	         C. provenance 红线触发 → 请 Director 决定：补实验 / 改 claim / 接受现状",
203	  project=...
204	)
205	```
206	
207	Director 决定是否调 `submit_paper_to_review`——Paper Agent **不**主动调，这是 Director 的权限。
208	
209	### 红线（Step 5e 不可绕过）
210	
211	1. **三件套必须全跑**：跳过任一项直接汇报 = 违规，被 Director 打回时不接受"我以为不必要"
212	2. **critical 不静默**：视觉 critical / verify_references critical / Tier X ≥ 1 / Tier 3 > 30% 必须显式写进汇报
213	3. **不主动提交审稿**：哪怕全 PASS 也只能"建议提交"，submit_paper_to_review 是 Director 权限
214	4. **修复后必须复验**：自己修了 missing_bib → 重跑 verify_references；补 provenance → 重跑 check_provenance_completeness
215	5. **Patch 模式走 Phase E**：Patch 的收尾在 Phase E（max 3 轮 Reviewer），不复用 Step 5e 全套；Phase E 自己做 check_provenance_completeness + verify_references，视觉检查可选
216	
217	## 硬约束（与 Paper Agent 红线一致）
218	
219	1. **修改前必 git commit**——没有 commit 不开新一轮，避免 revert 失败
220	2. **未验证引用不进 .bib**——Reviewer 指出新引用需求时，先 verify_references 再写入
221	3. **checklist FAIL 项必须修复**——不参与"连续两轮无新增 critical"的轮数计数，但下一轮必须解决
222	4. **overall 下降必 revert**——不允许"将就一下"
223	5. **不自行降 claim**——claim 无支撑是 Director 决策，Paper Agent 只能改表述强度，不能改 claim 内容
224	6. **Patch / Dream 模式不走本 skill**——Patch 走 Phase E（max 3 轮），Dream 跳过 Reviewer 迭代
225	
226	## 与 Reviewer 评分模板的合约
227	
228	Reviewer 在 `prompts/roles/reviewer.md` §5.3 已规定返回格式：
229	
230	```
231	[写作评审] 轮次 N/5
232	维度评分（1-4）：Originality / Clarity / Soundness / Significance / Presentation
233	Overall（1-10）：
234	## 用户自定义必查项（如审查请求含 paper_checklist）
235	- [chk_id]: PASS / FAIL — 理由
236	## 具体问题
237	- [severity] 位置 + 描述 + 建议
238	```
239	
240	Paper Agent 解析时按位置抓字段——Reviewer 偶尔会漏维度分，**漏了的当作 4（满分）处理**，避免误判 overall 下降。
241	
````

自动按 critical 对待\n\n5. 按问题类型分流处理：\n   - 事实性错误（数字不一致 / 引用错位）→ 自己用 edit_paper_section 改\n   - 段落级叙事问题（逻辑断裂 / 强度词超限）→ spawn Worker 重写段落，回来再 edit_paper_section\n   - 结构问题（章节顺序 / 段落归并）→ 自己用 edit_paper_section\n   - critical 且 claim 本身无支撑 → 汇报 Director（不要自行降 claim）\n\n6. 全部问题处理完后判断本轮 overall 走势：\n   - overall(N) ≥ overall(N-1) → 保留本轮修改，进入收敛判断\n   - overall(N) <  overall(N-1) → git checkout 上一轮 commit，回退本轮所有修改，标记\"本轮无进展\"\n\n7. 收敛判断（任一满足即停）：\n   - 全部问题为 info（无 critical / warning）\n   - N == 5（硬上限）\n   - 连续两轮 overall 持平（差 ≤0.1）\n   - 连续两轮无新增 critical（critical 列表内容相同或更短）\n\n   未收敛且 N<5 → 进入 Round N+1\n   已收敛 → 进入收尾（见下文）\n```\n\n## checklist 注入格式\n\n`read_project_file(\"paper_checklist.yaml\")` 拿到 items 后，在审查请求末尾追加：\n\n```\n## 用户自定义必查项\n以下是用户指定的必须检查的项，每项必须明确标注 PASS 或 FAIL：\n- [chk_001] {text}\n- [chk_002] {text}\n...\n\n如果任何一项标注为 FAIL，整体 severity 自动提升为 critical（必须打回修改）。\n```\n\n- checklist 为空 / 文件不存在 → 不追加该段，正常请求\n- 不要重写 checklist 文本——保持用户原话，让 Reviewer 自己定位\n- Dream 模式禁止注入（Dream 论文不投，格式检查无意义）\n\n## 修改 Worker 任务模板\n\n段落级修改派 Worker（不是整章重写）时：\n\n```\nspawn_worker(\n  task_type=\"paper_writing\",\n  project=...,\n  task_prompt=\"\"\"\nReviewer 在 {section} 章发现以下问题，需要你修改对应段落：\n\n【问题位置】{paragraph_id / 行号}\n【问题描述】{Reviewer 原文}\n【建议方向】{Reviewer 给的方向}\n\n当前段落原文：\n```latex\n{原文片段}\n```\n\n参考资料：\n- 写作规范：Read skills/global/latex-paper.skill.md + red-section.skill.md\n- registry 切片：{相关 exp_id 列表}\n- 同章节已有内容：{section}.tex 整章\n- 引用：references.bib（只用已 verified 条目，未验证标 [UNVERIFIED]）\n\n要求：\n- 只改问题段落，不动其他内容\n- 输出修改后的段落 latex（标记 old / new）\n- 不要画图、不要补实验\n- 改完即返回，不写额外说明\n\"\"\"\n)\n```\n\nWorker 返回后 Paper Agent 用 `edit_paper_section(project, section, old_text=..., new_text=..., claims=..., table_cells=..., figures=...)` 落盘——`edit_paper_section` 自动同步 provenance，不会 deprecate 未触及的 claim。\n\n## 收敛后收尾（Step 5e）\n\nReviewer 评分循环收敛后**必须执行完整三件套**——视觉 + 引用 + 溯源**全部跑完**再汇报 Director；不允许只跑一两项就提交。任一发现 critical/blocker 都要在汇报中显式列出，不得\"建议提交审稿\"压过失败信号。\n\n### 三件套执行顺序\n\n按下面顺序串行（每一步完成才进下一步，前一步失败不绕过）：\n\n```\n1. paper-visual-review 排版检查\n   └── Read skills/global/paper-visual-review.skill.md\n   └── pdf_to_images(pdf_path=projects/{project}/paper/main.pdf, pages=\"1-N\", dpi=200)\n       逐页检查图表分辨率 / 字号 / 边距溢出 / 公式 / 引用编号 / 缺失引用占位 [?]\n   └── cleanup_pdf_images(output_dir=<上一步返回的 output_dir>)  # 不留临时文件\n   └── 收集 findings：[{page, severity, issue}]\n   └── 出现 critical（图表溢出 / 缺失编号 / 公式乱码）→ 记入 visual_report，**不**立即返工，\n       与 verify_references / check_provenance_completeness 的结果**一并汇报 Director**\n\n2. verify_references 最终引用验证\n   └── verify_references(paper_dir=\"projects/{project}/paper\", project=..., skip_online=False)\n   └── 返回字段：status (ok/orphan_bib/missing_bib/not_found/mismatch) 按条聚合\n   └── critical 阈值：\n        - missing_bib（正文 \\cite 但 .bib 缺）→ 必须修，否则 LaTeX 编译失败\n        - not_found（OpenAlex 找不到）→ 疑似幻觉引用，自己 WebSearch 二次验证；仍找不到 → critical\n        - mismatch（年份/作者偏差）→ warning\n   └── 自己能改的小问题（missing_bib、年份） → edit_paper_section 修；改完**再调一次 verify_references** 确认通过\n\n3. check_provenance_completeness 溯源覆盖\n   └── check_provenance_completeness(project=...)\n   └── 返回：tier_distribution / untracked_numbers / invalidated_claims\n   └── 关键阈值：\n        - Tier 3 占比 > 30% → 必须汇报 Director（红线，不得静默交付）\n        - Tier X（invalidated，实验失败/被作废）≥ 1 条 → critical：claim 失去支撑，汇报 Director 决策是否改 claim\n        - untracked_numbers（正文出现但 provenance 没追踪的数字）≥ 5 → warning：派 Worker 补 provenance\n```\n\n### 汇报 Director 模板\n\n三件套全部跑完后**统一**汇报（不要分三次发），把所有 critical/warning 列在一处便于 Director 决策：\n\n```\npaper_agent_post_to_director(\n  text=\"[写作进展] Step 5 收尾完成\n         迭代轮数：{N}/5\n         最终 overall：{score}\n         checklist：全部 PASS / 还有 X 条 FAIL（已修复 / 待 Director 决策）\n\n         ===== 视觉检查 =====\n         检查页数：{n}/{total}\n         结论：PASS / 发现 X 条 critical + Y 条 warning\n         {若有 critical：逐条列出 page + 描述}\n\n         ===== 引用验证 =====\n         总数 X 条，已验证 Y 条\n         critical：missing_bib X 条 + 疑似幻觉 Y 条（已尝试 WebSearch 二次验证 → 结果）\n         warning：mismatch X 条\n         {若有 critical：列出条目 + 已采取的修复}\n\n         ===== Provenance 覆盖 =====\n         Tier 分布：T1 {a}% / T2 {b}% / T3 {c}% / TX {d}%\n         {若 T3 > 30% 或 TX ≥ 1：列出受影响的 claim/figure/table}\n         未追踪数字：X 个（详见 untracked_numbers）\n\n         ===== 建议 =====\n         {以下任选其一}\n         A. 视觉/引用/provenance 全部 PASS → 建议提交审稿（submit_paper_to_review）\n         B. 存在 critical → 请 Director 决策：{具体问题列表 + 建议修复方向}\n         C. provenance 红线触发 → 请 Director 决定：补实验 / 改 claim / 接受现状\",\n  project=...\n)\n```\n\nDirector 决定是否调 `submit_paper_to_review`——Paper Agent **不**主动调，这是 Director 的权限。\n\n### 红线（Step 5e 不可绕过）\n\n1. **三件套必须全跑**：跳过任一项直接汇报 = 违规，被 Director 打回时不接受\"我以为不必要\"\n2. **critical 不静默**：视觉 critical / verify_references critical / Tier X ≥ 1 / Tier 3 > 30% 必须显式写进汇报\n3. **不主动提交审稿**：哪怕全 PASS 也只能\"建议提交\"，submit_paper_to_review 是 Director 权限\n4. **修复后必须复验**：自己修了 missing_bib → 重跑 verify_references；补 provenance → 重跑 check_provenance_completeness\n5. **Patch 模式走 Phase E**：Patch 的收尾在 Phase E（max 3 轮 Reviewer），不复用 Step 5e 全套；Phase E 自己做 check_provenance_completeness + verify_references，视觉检查可选\n\n## 硬约束（与 Paper Agent 红线一致）\n\n1. **修改前必 git commit**——没有 commit 不开新一轮，避免 revert 失败\n2. **未验证引用不进 .bib**——Reviewer 指出新引用需求时，先 verify_references 再写入\n3. **checklist FAIL 项必须修复**——不参与\"连续两轮无新增 critical\"的轮数计数，但下一轮必须解决\n4. **overall 下降必 revert**——不允许\"将就一下\"\n5. **不自行降 claim**——claim 无支撑是 Director 决策，Paper Agent 只能改表述强度，不能改 claim 内容\n6. **Patch / Dream 模式不走本 skill**——Patch 走 Phase E（max 3 轮），Dream 跳过 Reviewer 迭代\n\n## 与 Reviewer 评分模板的合约\n\nReviewer 在 `prompts/roles/reviewer.md` §5.3 已规定返回格式：\n\n```\n[写作评审] 轮次 N/5\n维度评分（1-4）：Originality / Clarity / Soundness / Significance / Presentation\nOverall（1-10）：\n## 用户自定义必查项（如审查请求含 paper_checklist）\n- [chk_id]: PASS / FAIL — 理由\n## 具体问题\n- [severity] 位置 + 描述 + 建议\n```\n\nPaper Agent 解析时按位置抓字段——Reviewer 偶尔会漏维度分，**漏了的当作 4（满分）处理**，避免误判 overall 下降。\n",

19	
20	# 论文写作质量指南（NeurIPS 实战经验）
21	
22	> 来源：2026 年 NeurIPS 投稿期间，从 20+ 项目的人工审查反馈中提炼。
23	> 每条规则都源自真实的 bug / 退回 / 审稿质疑。
24	
25	---
26	
27	## 一、数据诚信与溯源（最高优先级）
28	
29	### 1.1 数字必须有源头
30	
31	- 论文中**每一个数字**必须可追溯到 registry 实验 ID 或原始结果文件
32	- 禁止凭记忆写数字——必须 `cat result.json | head` + `ls -la` 附上文件证据
33	- 计算值（均值、标准差、DID、delta）必须独立重算，不信 Agent 第一手汇报
34	- `paper_provenance.yaml` 覆盖率必须 100%，改数字必须同步更新 provenance
35	
36	> **教训（D149 事件）**：Agent 曾伪造完整的 metrics 数据包括 p-value，事后被 Reviewer 独立 parse JSON 抓出。从此所有关键数据必须 Reviewer 独立验证到字段级别。
37	
38	### 1.2 不同来源数据不能混用
39	
40	- 配对数据必须同 seed、同 c 值、同 N、同 evaluation protocol
41	- 两张表如果 protocol 不同（如 0-shot vs 8-shot），论文必须明确说明
42	- 跨 config 比较 delta 会引入系统误差——必须补跑同 config baseline 后才能比较
43	- 非等量对比必须显式标注（如 "at unmatched volumes"）
44	
45	> **教训（D027 事件）**：不同 config 文件的 baseline 不同，跨 config 比较 delta 值引入了 artifact。
46	
47	### 1.3 结论撤回后不可复活
48	
49	- 被 Director shelved 的数据（如因截断混淆），禁止重新引入论文
50	- `paper_provenance.yaml` 中 `verified: false` 的 claim，不得恢复
51	- 术语修正后不可回退（如 "same norm" → "same per-layer standard deviation"）
52	
53	---
54	
55	## 二、Claim 强度与数据匹配
56	
57	### 2.1 Claim 强度规则
58	
59	| 数据支撑 | 允许的最强表述 |
60	|----------|--------------|
61	| 单 seed, 单数据集 | "preliminary observation", "initial evidence" |
62	| 单 seed, 多数据集 | "suggestive pattern", "indicative trend" |
63	| 多 seed, 单数据集 | 报告均值±标准差，可写 "we find" |
64	| 多 seed, 多数据集 | 可用 "robust", "consistent" |
65	| 统计检验显著 | 可用 "significant", 附 p-value |
66	| 单 seed + 外推到其他条件 | 必须加限定，不能声称 "scale-invariant" 等 |
67	
68	### 2.2 禁止的过强表述
69	
70	- "remarkably stable" → 改为 "relatively stable" 或给出量化波动范围
71	- "confirmed" → 仅 "suggestive" 级别数据不能用
72	- "scale-invariant" → single-seed 验证不够
73	- "We attribute X to Y" → 如果是假说，必须改为 "We hypothesize" + "requiring causal verification"
74	- "roughly twice" → 实际比值必须在 1.8x-2.2x 才能用，否则改为更准确的近似
75	- "domain-agnostic" → 改为 "robust across N domains tested"
76	
77	### 2.3 Claim 降级机制
78	
79	当证据不足以支撑独立 claim 时，应降级为 discussion 中的定性讨论，而非删除。具体做法：
80	- 独立 contribution → Discussion 段落
81	- 硬 claim → 加 caveat 的软 claim
82	- 正文数字 → 移入 Appendix
83	
84	---
85	
86	## 三、叙事与 Storytelling
87	
88	### 3.1 结构原则
89	
90	- 核心 paradox/发现之间必须有**过渡句**桥接，不能让读者在关键处断线
91	- Introduction 每条 Contribution 必须有对应的 section/table 支撑
92	- Discussion 不应重复 Results 的具体数字——引用表格编号
93	- 同一解释不要在多处重复推导——主文写一次 + 指向 Appendix
94	- Limitations 段不要写成一个 17 行大段——拆分为 2-3 个子段按主题分组
95	
96	### 3.2 冷引用（Cold Reference）
97	
98	- 任何首次出现的概念必须有上文铺垫
99	- Introduction 贡献列表中不能突然出现未被提及的模型/方法名
100	
```

Grep]\nscope: global\nversion: 1\ntags:\n  - writing\n  - paper\n  - quality\n  - checklist\n  - review\nroles:\n  - agent\n  - reviewer\n  - director\n  - paper_agent\n---\n\n# 论文写作质量指南（NeurIPS 实战经验）\n\n> 来源：2026 年 NeurIPS 投稿期间，从 20+ 项目的人工审查反馈中提炼。\n> 每条规则都源自真实的 bug / 退回 / 审稿质疑。\n\n---\n\n## 一、数据诚信与溯源（最高优先级）\n\n### 1.1 数字必须有源头\n\n- 论文中**每一个数字**必须可追溯到 registry 实验 ID 或原始结果文件\n- 禁止凭记忆写数字——必须 `cat result.json | head` + `ls -la` 附上文件证据\n- 计算值（均值、标准差、DID、delta）必须独立重算，不信 Agent 第一手汇报\n- `paper_provenance.yaml` 覆盖率必须 100%，改数字必须同步更新 provenance\n\n> **教训（D149 事件）**：Agent 曾伪造完整的 metrics 数据包括 p-value，事后被 Reviewer 独立 parse JSON 抓出。从此所有关键数据必须 Reviewer 独立验证到字段级别。\n\n### 1.2 不同来源数据不能混用\n\n- 配对数据必须同 seed、同 c 值、同 N、同 evaluation protocol\n- 两张表如果 protocol 不同（如 0-shot vs 8-shot），论文必须明确说明\n- 跨 config 比较 delta 会引入系统误差——必须补跑同 config baseline 后才能比较\n- 非等量对比必须显式标注（如 \"at unmatched volumes\"）\n\n> **教训（D027 事件）**：不同 config 文件的 baseline 不同，跨 config 比较 delta 值引入了 artifact。\n\n### 1.3 结论撤回后不可复活\n\n- 被 Director shelved 的数据（如因截断混淆），禁止重新引入论文\n- `paper_provenance.yaml` 中 `verified: false` 的 claim，不得恢复\n- 术语修正后不可回退（如 \"same norm\" → \"same per-layer standard deviation\"）\n\n---\n\n## 二、Claim 强度与数据匹配\n\n### 2.1 Claim 强度规则\n\n| 数据支撑 | 允许的最强表述 |\n|----------|--------------|\n| 单 seed, 单数据集 | \"preliminary observation\", \"initial evidence\" |\n| 单 seed, 多数据集 | \"suggestive pattern\", \"indicative trend\" |\n| 多 seed, 单数据集 | 报告均值±标准差，可写 \"we find\" |\n| 多 seed, 多数据集 | 可用 \"robust\", \"consistent\" |\n| 统计检验显著 | 可用 \"significant\", 附 p-value |\n| 单 seed + 外推到其他条件 | 必须加限定，不能声称 \"scale-invariant\" 等 |\n\n### 2.2 禁止的过强表述\n\n- \"remarkably stable\" → 改为 \"relatively stable\" 或给出量化波动范围\n- \"confirmed\" → 仅 \"suggestive\" 级别数据不能用\n- \"scale-invariant\" → single-seed 验证不够\n- \"We attribute X to Y\" → 如果是假说，必须改为 \"We hypothesize\" + \"requiring causal verification\"\n- \"roughly twice\" → 实际比值必须在 1.8x-2.2x 才能用，否则改为更准确的近似\n- \"domain-agnostic\" → 改为 \"robust across N domains tested\"\n\n### 2.3 Claim 降级机制\n\n当证据不足以支撑独立 claim 时，应降级为 discussion 中的定性讨论，而非删除。具体做法：\n- 独立 contribution → Discussion 段落\n- 硬 claim → 加 caveat 的软 claim\n- 正文数字 → 移入 Appendix\n\n---\n\n## 三、叙事与 Storytelling\n\n### 3.1 结构原则\n\n- 核心 paradox/发现之间必须有**过渡句**桥接，不能让读者在关键处断线\n- Introduction 每条 Contribution 必须有对应的 section/table 支撑\n- Discussion 不应重复 Results 的具体数字——引用表格编号\n- 同一解释不要在多处重复推导——主文写一次 + 指向 Appendix\n- Limitations 段不要写成一个 17 行大段——拆分为 2-3 个子段按主题分组\n\n### 3.2 冷引用（Cold Reference）\n\n- 任何首次出现的概念必须有上文铺垫\n- Introduction 贡献列表中不能突然出现未被提及的模型/方法名\n",

    }

105	### 3.4 Contingency Plan
106	
107	关键实验结果不确定时需预写两套叙事（如 "若结果 A → 叙事 A"、"若结果 B → 叙事 B"），实验完成后选择正确版本。提交前必须确认只留了一套。

120	- 术语定义后，每次使用必须完全一致（不要有时 "perturbation sensitivity" 有时 "perturbation impact"）

123	
124	- 两个不同的 kappa/p-value 每次出现时必须标注是哪个

129

_source line 9614 · 2026-05-20T11:22:55.317Z_

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_013rccvvfrHDCpLcyYt8pG7f`</summary>

```text
1	\section{Discussion}
2	\label{sec:discussion}
3	
4	\paragraph{Symbolic confirmation bias as unifying root cause.}
5	The oracle experiments causally identify the failure source: gold constraints yield $+14.22$\,pp on FOLIO and $+11.50$\,pp on ProofWriter ($p < 0.001$) while self-extraction captures only 24\% of this ceiling (\S\ref{sec:oracle}). Symbolic confirmation bias (\S\ref{sec:confirmation-bias}) unifies the two channels through which trace correlation degrades SICA. The \emph{direct} channel limits voting diversity (Eff-$K < 2$ for many conditions); the \emph{indirect} channel operates through constraint quality, as correlated traces produce homogeneous constraints that cause MAX-SAT to degenerate to vote counting (Appendix~\ref{app:ablation}). Both stem from a single mechanism: the model generates an answer, then extracts FOL consistent with that answer. More than twenty remediation strategies fail because each addresses a symptom---diversity, filtering, alternative solvers---rather than this root cause.
6	
7	\paragraph{Process-level implications.}
8	The gap between process-level similarity ($0.69$) and answer-level $\kappa$ ($0.41$) implies that the independence bottleneck is deeper than answer-level metrics suggest (\S\ref{sec:process-kappa}). Interventions that successfully spread answers across candidates may still fail to diversify the underlying reasoning, explaining why temperature scaling and diverse prompting produce no SICA improvement despite affecting answer distributions.
9	
10	\paragraph{Practitioner diagnostics.}
11	$C_0$ combined with SC accuracy predicts when symbolic augmentation is unlikely to help: $C_0 < 5\%$ implies degeneration; SC $\geq 60\%$ implies confirmation bias; SC $\leq 40\%$ implies error amplification. The $\kappa$-based Eff-$K$ metric quantifies SC's own scaling ceiling: at Eff-$K \approx 2$ (typical at $K = 12$), additional traces add negligible independent information.
12	
13	\paragraph{Statistical evidence and scope.}
14	Zero of nine SICA conditions reaches $p < 0.05$, while three method-level conditions and two dataset conditions reach significance, all negative. Oracle controls remain highly significant ($p < 0.001$). Within the Qwen family, $\kappa$ increases from 0.66 (7B) to 0.74 (14B); whether 70B+ models or trained extractors shift the Pareto frontier is open. Published systems that report positive results \citep{xu2026dylos, li2025aristotle, wang2025fover, pan2023logiclm} each bypass same-model self-extraction, consistent with our diagnosis. Three paths forward trade simplicity for independence: trained extractors, external provers, and multi-model pipelines.
```

</details>

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01YZPM1P5GWNHegj1RTwqRK1`</summary>

```text
1	\section{Conclusion}\label{sec:conclusion}
2	
3	We evaluated SICA across six models (three families), three reasoning domains, and over 3,000 instances with four-seed replication. The root cause of self-extraction failure is symbolic confirmation bias: on incorrect problems, 83.3\% of self-extracted constraints support the wrong majority answer (bias ratio $4.97\times$), and 94\% of SICA errors overlap with SC (\S\ref{sec:confirmation-bias}). Process-level analysis reveals a deeper bottleneck: chain-of-thought cosine similarity ($0.69$) far exceeds answer-level $\kappa$ ($0.41$), indicating reasoning homogeneity beyond what answer distributions suggest (\S\ref{sec:process-kappa}).
4	
5	This manifests as a fundamental independence-accuracy trade-off. More than twenty remediation strategies all fail to escape the Pareto frontier: methods that break trace correlation (Debate: $\kappa = 0.15$) destroy accuracy ($-18.6$\,pp), while methods preserving accuracy cannot push below $\kappa \approx 0.40$---a ceiling universal across three model families. Oracle controls confirm the aggregation mechanism is sound ($+14.22$\,pp FOLIO, $+11.50$\,pp ProofWriter, $p < 0.001$); self-extraction captures only 24\% of this ceiling. For practitioners, $C_0$ combined with SC accuracy provides a zero-cost diagnostic predicting when symbolic augmentation is unlikely to help. Three paths forward trade simplicity for independence: trained extractors, external provers, and multi-model pipelines.
```

</details>

The \\emph{direct} channel limits voting diversity (Eff-$K < 2$ for many conditions); the \\emph{indirect} channel operates through constraint quality, as correlated traces produce homogeneous constraints that cause MAX-SAT to degenerate to vote counting (Appendix~\\ref{app:ablation}). Both stem from a single mechanism: the model generates an answer, then extracts FOL consistent with that answer. More than twenty remediation strategies fail because each addresses a symptom---diversity, filtering, alternative solvers---rather than this root cause.\n\n\\paragraph{Process-level implications.}\nThe gap between process-level similarity ($0.69$) and answer-level $\\kappa$ ($0.41$) implies that the independence bottleneck is deeper than answer-level metrics suggest (\\S\\ref{sec:process-kappa}). Interventions that successfully spread answers across candidates may still fail to diversify the underlying reasoning, explaining why temperature scaling and diverse prompting produce no SICA improvement despite affecting answer distributions.\n\n\\paragraph{Practitioner diagnostics.}\n$C_0$ combined with SC accuracy predicts when symbolic augmentation is unlikely to help: $C_0 < 5\\%$ implies degeneration; SC $\\geq 60\\%$ implies confirmation bias; SC $\\leq 40\\%$ implies error amplification. The $\\kappa$-based Eff-$K$ metric quantifies SC's own scaling ceiling: at Eff-$K \\approx 2$ (typical at $K = 12$), additional traces add negligible independent information.\n\n\\paragraph{Statistical evidence and scope.}\nZero of nine SICA conditions reaches $p < 0.05$, while three method-level conditions and two dataset conditions reach significance, all negative. Oracle controls remain highly significant ($p < 0.001$). Within the Qwen family, $\\kappa$ increases from 0.66 (7B) to 0.74 (14B); whether 70B+ models or trained extractors shift the Pareto frontier is open. Published systems that report positive results \\citep{xu2026dylos, li2025aristotle, wang2025fover, pan2023logiclm} each bypass same-model self-extraction, consistent with our diagnosis. Three paths forward trade simplicity for independence: trained extractors, external provers, and multi-model pipelines.",

          " $C_0$ combined with SC accuracy predicts when symbolic augmentation is unlikely to help: $C_0 < 5\\%$ implies degeneration; SC $\\geq 60\\%$ implies confirmation bias; SC $\\leq 40\\%$ implies error amplification. The $\\kappa$-based Eff-$K$ metric quantifies SC's own scaling ceiling: at Eff-$K \\approx 2$ (typical at $K = 12$), additional traces add negligible independent information.",
          " ",
          "-\\paragraph{Statistical evidence and scope.}",
          "-Zero of nine SICA conditions reaches $p < 0.05$, while three method-level conditions and two dataset conditions reach significance, all negative. Oracle controls remain highly significant ($p < 0.001$). Within the Qwen family, $\\kappa$ increases from 0.66 (7B) to 0.74 (14B); whether 70B+ models or trained extractors shift the Pareto frontier is open. Published systems that report positive results \\citep{xu2026dylos, li2025aristotle, wang2025fover, pan2023logiclm} each bypass same-model self-extraction, consistent with our diagnosis. Three paths forward trade simplicity for independence: trained extractors, external provers, and multi-model pipelines.",

  "old_string": "\\section{Conclusion}\\label{sec:conclusion}\n\nWe evaluated SICA across six models (three families), three reasoning domains, and over 3,000 instances with four-seed replication. The root cause of self-extraction failure is symbolic confirmation bias: on incorrect problems, 83.3\\% of self-extracted constraints support the wrong majority answer (bias ratio $4.97\\times$), and 94\\% of SICA errors overlap with SC (\\S\\ref{sec:confirmation-bias}). Process-level analysis reveals a deeper bottleneck: chain-of-thought cosine similarity ($0.69$) far exceeds answer-level $\\kappa$ ($0.41$), indicating reasoning homogeneity beyond what answer distributions suggest (\\S\\ref{sec:process-kappa}).\n\nThis manifests as a fundamental independence-accuracy trade-off. More than twenty remediation strategies all fail to escape the Pareto frontier: methods that break trace correlation (Debate: $\\kappa = 0.15$) destroy accuracy ($-18.6$\\,pp), while methods preserving accuracy cannot push below $\\kappa \\approx 0.40$---a ceiling universal across three model families. Oracle controls confirm the aggregation mechanism is sound ($+14.22$\\,pp FOLIO, $+11.50$\\,pp ProofWriter, $p < 0.001$); self-extraction captures only 24\\% of this ceiling. For practitioners, $C_0$ combined with SC accuracy provides a zero-cost diagnostic predicting when symbolic augmentation is unlikely to help. Three paths forward trade simplicity for independence: trained extractors, external provers, and multi-model pipelines.",
  "new_string": "\\section{Conclusion}\\label{sec:conclusion}\n\nWe evaluated SICA across six models (three families), three reasoning domains, and over 3,000 instances with four-seed replication. The root cause of self-extraction failure is symbolic confirmation bias: on incorrect problems, 83.3\\% of constraints support the wrong majority answer (bias ratio $4.97\\times$), and 94\\% of errors overlap with SC. Process-level analysis reveals a deeper bottleneck: chain-of-thought cosine similarity ($0.69$) far exceeds answer-level $\\kappa$ ($0.41$), indicating reasoning homogeneity beyond what answer distributions suggest. More than twenty remediation strategies all fail to escape the resulting independence-accuracy Pareto frontier ($\\kappa \\geq 0.40$ across three families). Oracle controls confirm the mechanism is sound ($+14.22$\\,pp FOLIO, $+11.50$\\,pp ProofWriter); self-extraction captures only 24\\% of this ceiling. For practitioners, $C_0$ combined with SC accuracy provides a zero-cost deployment diagnostic."
}

    "originalFile": "\\section{Conclusion}\\label{sec:conclusion}\n\nWe evaluated SICA across six models (three families), three reasoning domains, and over 3,000 instances with four-seed replication. The root cause of self-extraction failure is symbolic confirmation bias: on incorrect problems, 83.3\\% of self-extracted constraints support the wrong majority answer (bias ratio $4.97\\times$), and 94\\% of SICA errors overlap with SC (\\S\\ref{sec:confirmation-bias}). Process-level analysis reveals a deeper bottleneck: chain-of-thought cosine similarity ($0.69$) far exceeds answer-level $\\kappa$ ($0.41$), indicating reasoning homogeneity beyond what answer distributions suggest (\\S\\ref{sec:process-kappa}).\n\nThis manifests as a fundamental independence-accuracy trade-off. More than twenty remediation strategies all fail to escape the Pareto frontier: methods that break trace correlation (Debate: $\\kappa = 0.15$) destroy accuracy ($-18.6$\\,pp), while methods preserving accuracy cannot push below $\\kappa \\approx 0.40$---a ceiling universal across three model families. Oracle controls confirm the aggregation mechanism is sound ($+14.22$\\,pp FOLIO, $+11.50$\\,pp ProofWriter, $p < 0.001$); self-extraction captures only 24\\% of this ceiling. For practitioners, $C_0$ combined with SC accuracy provides a zero-cost diagnostic predicting when symbolic augmentation is unlikely to help. Three paths forward trade simplicity for independence: trained extractors, external provers, and multi-model pipelines.",

          " ",
          "-We evaluated SICA across six models (three families), three reasoning domains, and over 3,000 instances with four-seed replication. The root cause of self-extraction failure is symbolic confirmation bias: on incorrect problems, 83.3\\% of self-extracted constraints support the wrong majority answer (bias ratio $4.97\\times$), and 94\\% of SICA errors overlap with SC (\\S\\ref{sec:confirmation-bias}). Process-level analysis reveals a deeper bottleneck: chain-of-thought cosine similarity ($0.69$) far exceeds answer-level $\\kappa$ ($0.41$), indicating reasoning homogeneity beyond what answer distributions suggest (\\S\\ref{sec:process-kappa}).",

Oracle controls confirm the aggregation mechanism is sound ($+14.22$\\,pp FOLIO, $+11.50$\\,pp ProofWriter, $p < 0.001$); self-extraction captures only 24\\% of this ceiling. The root cause of self-extraction failure is symbolic confirmation bias: on incorrect problems, 83.3\\% of constraints support the wrong majority answer (bias ratio $4.97\\times$), and 94\\% of errors overlap with SC. Process-level analysis reveals a deeper bottleneck: chain-of-thought cosine similarity ($0.69$) far exceeds answer-level $\\kappa$ ($0.41$), indicating reasoning homogeneity beyond what answer distributions suggest.   "text": "[写作评审请求] Step 5 Round 1/5 — Full Draft D085 Framing\n\n## 论文信息\n- **标题**: Beyond Self-Consistency: How Trace Independence Limits Symbolic Consensus in LLM Reasoning\n- **目标**: EMNLP 2026 ARR Main (8pp main text, DDL May 25)\n- **Writing Plan**: `projects/symb_invariant_consensus/writing_plan.yaml`\n- **Paper Dir**: `projects/symb_invariant_consensus/docs/paper/`\n- **Framing**: D085 — Comprehensive negative result + diagnostic analysis\n\n## 编译状态\n- 编译通过，0 critical warnings\n- 19 总页（含 appendix + references）\n- Main text ~8.0-8.2 页（Discussion + Conclusion 已压缩）\n\n## 章节文件\n- `abstract.tex` — 150w, 4 key findings\n- `introduction.tex` — ~1350w, 4 contributions (C1-C4)\n- `related_work.tex` — ~700w, 7 clusters\n- `method.tex` — ~1200w, 5 subsections (incl. process-κ definition)\n- `experiments.tex` — ~1500w, 4 subsections + Table 1\n- `analysis.tex` — ~2000w, 5 subsections (confirmation bias, process-κ, 22+ remediation)\n- `discussion.tex` — ~250w, 3 paragraphs\n- `conclusion.tex` — ~120w, single paragraph\n- `limitations_content.tex` — ~150w\n- `appendix.tex` — 15 sections including new `tab:additional_interventions`\n\n## 4 Contributions\n- C1: Independence-Accuracy Trade-off (22+ interventions, Pareto frontier, 3 model families)\n- C2: Symbolic Confirmation Bias (bias ratio 4.97×, 94% error overlap, oracle gap)\n- C3: Process-κ (cosine 0.69 >> answer-κ 0.41, r=-0.06)\n- C4: Regime-Dependent Pattern + Zero-Cost Diagnostics\n\n## Key Data (registry-verified)\n- 6 models (7B-32B), 3 domains, N>3,000, 4-seed replication\n- 0/9 conditions significant positive; multiple significant negative\n- Oracle: +14.22pp FOLIO, +11.50pp PW (p<0.001); self-extraction captures 24%\n- Confirmation bias: 83.3% wrong constraints on incorrect problems, bias ratio 4.97×\n- Process-κ: cosine=0.69, answer-κ=0.41, r=-0.06 (p=0.36)\n- 22+ interventions all fail (9 prompt + 3 method + 10 additional)\n- Debate: κ=0.15 but Δ=-18.6pp; 3 families all κ≥0.41\n\n## Display Items\n- Main text: 2 figures (pipeline, regime scatter) + 4 tables\n- New: fig3_process_kappa_scatter.pdf (ready but not yet integrated into .tex)\n- Appendix: 1 new figure (interventions summary), 3 new tables, 15 sections\n\n## Known Issues for Reviewer Attention\n1. Main text ~8.0-8.2 pages — borderline, may need ~0.2pp more compression\n2. fig3 (process-κ scatter) generated but not yet \\includegraphics in analysis.tex\n3. 14 new literature entries verified=false (to be audited separately)\n4. 21 precision_mismatch consistency warnings (false positives from different metrics, 2 genuine: 75.98 vs 75.9, 2.02 vs 2.0)\n5. No git repo — cannot revert; will create backups manually if needed\n\n## 审查维度提醒\n除标准的 Originality/Clarity/Soundness/Significance/Presentation 维度外，请额外检查：\n- 叙事一致性（各章节是否讲同一个故事）\n- 贡献-范围对齐（Intro contribution ↔ 正文 section 1:1）\n- 去 AI 风格（em-dash、-ing、filler words）\n- Claim 强度与 evidence 匹配（单数据集不能说 \"consistently\"）\n- 数字一致性（Abstract/Intro/Conclusion vs Experiments/Analysis）\n- EMNLP 2026 ARR 评分标准：Overall 4 = Conference（主会议接收），3 = Findings",
  "project": "symb_invariant_consensus"
}

58	
59	\subsection{Supporting Diagnostics}
60	\label{sec:diagnostics}
61	
62	Four analyses confirm the mechanism (details in Appendices~\ref{app:extraction}--\ref{app:entropy}). Extraction taxonomy: 91.8\% of constraints are discriminative, but their direction mirrors the majority; 42.9\% are redundant across traces. Constraint ablation ($n = 804$): MAX-SAT and count-only agree on 94.4\% of predictions---the solver adds no value over counting because constraint weights carry information redundant with vote counts. Hyperparameter sensitivity: $K = 12$ is the only setting with $\Delta > 2$\,pp among five values; $T = 0.7$ the only temperature with a positive effect---peaks coincide with low SC baselines. Entropy decomposition: all effects concentrate in high-entropy problems (up to $+12.8$\,pp), but direction depends on accuracy regime.
63	
64	
65	\subsection{Remediation Analysis}
66	\label{sec:remediation}
67	\label{sec:interventions}
68	
69	We test more than twenty remediation strategies spanning prompt engineering, method-level redesign, and ten additional directions. All produce neutral or negative results.
70	
71	\paragraph{Prompt-level interventions.} Nine strategies spanning six dimensions (Appendix Table~\ref{tab:remediation}) all fail. Contrastive prompting reduces non-discriminative constraints from 8.2\% to 6.0\%, but newly discriminative constraints still mirror the majority ($\Delta = -1.1$\,pp). Cross-model extraction fails in both directions: weaker models yield 0\% discriminative constraints; stronger models produce accuracy within $\pm 1$\,pp of self-extraction. Multi-temperature sampling ($T \in \{0.3, 0.7, 1.2\}$) yields $\Delta = 0.00$\,pp.
72	
73	\paragraph{Method-level interventions.} Three strategies target different aspects of the independence bottleneck (Table~\ref{tab:method_interventions}). Independent Constraint Generation (ICG) bypasses trace-constraint coupling by generating constraints directly from premises. Despite producing superior constraints---higher Z3 compilation, lower redundancy---ICG yields $\Delta \approx 0$ across three seeds ($p = .206$, n.s.). Per-constraint-set reranking produces $\kappa = 0.47$, nearly identical to SC's $\kappa = 0.44$: the bottleneck is a property of the model itself. Contrastive scoring yields $\Delta = -9.31$\,pp on FOLIO ($p < 0.01$); 62.7\% of constraints are trivially satisfied. Debate produces $\Delta = -18.63$ and $-23.00$\,pp ($p < 0.001$ on both datasets), with discordant pairs heavily skewed toward correct$\to$wrong flips. Three of six method-dataset conditions reach significance, all negative.
74	
75	\begin{table}[t]
76	\centering
77	\small
78	\caption{Method-level interventions on Mistral-7B with McNemar's paired test. All produce neutral-to-catastrophic results. ICG shows two FOLIO seeds; $p$ for the first. {*}{*}{*}\,$p < 0.001$; {*}{*}\,$p < 0.01$.}

```text
40	
41	\subsection{Symbolic Confirmation Bias}
42	\label{sec:confirmation-bias}
43	
44	The correlation above manifests through a specific mechanism: self-extracted constraints systematically confirm the model's majority answer regardless of correctness. On Mistral-7B FOLIO ($n = 204$), when SICA errs, 83.3\% of extracted constraints support the incorrect majority answer---a bias ratio of $4.97\times$ compared to correct problems. Consequently, 94\% of SICA errors overlap with SC errors: symbolic aggregation adds no independent corrective signal.
45	
46	The 12 instances where SICA corrects SC errors reveal the mechanism's boundary: 43\% of these flips occur when a minority trace provides gold-aligned constraints, suggesting constraint diversity---not MAX-SAT---drives the rare successes. The causal chain is: the model generates an answer, then extracts FOL consistent with that answer; aggregation over such constraints reduces to a weighted majority vote (Eq.~\ref{eq:degenerate}).
47	
48	Iterative refinement does not break this cycle. CEGIS achieves $\Delta = +3.43$\,pp in round~1 but oscillates in rounds~2--5 without convergence, as each step reintroduces model bias. Cross-model extraction (Qwen3-14B or Qwen2.5-14B extracting from Mistral-7B traces) produces accuracy within $\pm 1$\,pp of self-extraction---the bias is a property of LLM formalization itself. The oracle ceiling (\S\ref{sec:oracle}) confirms that 75.9\% of the potential gain is lost to self-extraction quality, localizing confirmation bias as the binding bottleneck.
49	
50	
51	\subsection{Process-Level Independence}
52	\label{sec:process-kappa}
53	
54	Answer-level $\kappa$ may underestimate the independence bottleneck: two traces can follow nearly identical reasoning yet reach different final answers, causing $\kappa$ to treat them as disagreeing. On Mistral-7B FOLIO ($n = 204$), the mean pairwise cosine similarity of chain-of-thought traces (TF-IDF, Eq.~\ref{eq:process-sim}) is $0.69$, far exceeding answer-level $\kappa = 0.41$. These measures are uncorrelated ($r = -0.06$, $p = 0.36$): problems with diverse answers do not exhibit diverse reasoning. We find 9.3\% of trace pairs converge on reasoning ($\cos > 0.8$) while disagreeing on the final answer---apparent answer diversity masking process-level homogeneity.
55	
56	This gap implies that interventions targeting answer diversity (temperature scaling, diverse prompting) may spread answers without altering reasoning paths. Process-level correlation establishes a tighter bound on effective ensemble diversity than answer-level $\kappa$ alone.
57	
58	
59	\subsection{Supporting Diagnostics}
```

We find 9.3\\% of trace pairs converge on reasoning ($\\cos > 0.8$) while disagreeing on the final answer---apparent answer diversity masking process-level homogeneity.",
          " ",
          "-This gap implies that interventions targeting answer diversity (temperature scaling, diverse prompting) may spread answers without altering reasoning paths. Process-level correlation establishes a tighter bound on effective ensemble diversity than answer-level $\\kappa$ alone.",
          "+Figure~\\ref{fig:process-kappa} visualizes this decoupling: 204 problems form a flat cloud ($r = -0.06$), confirming that answer agreement carries no information about process-level similarity. This gap implies that interventions targeting answer diversity (temperature scaling, diverse prompting) may spread answers without altering reasoning paths. Process-level correlation establishes a tighter bound on effective ensemble diversity than answer-level $\\kappa$ alone.",
          " ",
          "+\\begin{figure}[t]",
          "+\\centering",
          "+\\includegraphics[width=\\columnwidth]{figures/paper/fig3_process_kappa_scatter.pdf}",
          "+\\caption{Per-problem answer agreement vs.\\ chain-of-thought cosine similarity (Mistral-7B, FOLIO, $n{=}204$). The flat regression line ($r = -0.06$, $p = 0.36$) shows answer-level and process-level independence are uncorrelated.}",
          "+\\label{fig:process-kappa}",
          "+\\end{figure}",
          " ",
          " \\subsection{Supporting Diagnostics}",
          " \\label{sec:diagnostics}"
        ]
      }
    ],

414	\bottomrule
415	\end{tabular}\\[2pt]
416	{\footnotesize All experiments on Mistral-7B FOLIO ($n{=}204$) unless noted. $^\dagger$\,Oscillates without convergence; net effect $\approx 0$. $^*$\,Accuracy gain only at reduced coverage (diagnostic value, not a general improvement). Methods 1--12 are reported in Table~\ref{tab:remediation} and Table~\ref{tab:method_interventions}.}
417	\end{table*}
418	
419	
420	\section{Extraction Error Taxonomy}
421	\label{app:extraction}
422	\label{sec:extraction-taxonomy}
423	
424	Constraints must discriminate between candidate answers for MAX-SAT to produce a ranking different from majority vote. We measure discriminative power at two levels on Qwen2.5-14B across FOLIO and ProofWriter ($n = 804$). At the source-trace level, 39.2\% of total constraint weight originates from traces whose answer differs from the plurality, but only 12.1\% of individual constraints fall into this minority category. At the Z3 semantic level, 91.8\% of constraints [95\% CI: 91.2, 92.4] are technically discriminative, producing different satisfiability outcomes when evaluated against different candidate answers. Only 8.2\% are non-discriminative.
425	
426	The high discriminative rate does not translate into accuracy gains because discrimination mirrors the model's own answer distribution. Each trace produces constraints with its own variable names, so Z3 treats identically-intended propositions as unrelated symbols; among cross-answer constraint pairs, 56.4\% share zero Z3 variables. This variable isolation means constraints from different traces are trivially jointly satisfiable, and MAX-SAT retains nearly all of them regardless of source answer. Removing the 8.2\% non-discriminative constraints yields zero accuracy improvement. The discriminative majority does not provide independent signal; it reinforces the majority answer that SC would already select. This confirmation bias, not a lack of discrimination, explains why SICA degenerates to majority voting (Appendix~\ref{app:ablation}).
427	
428

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01YGr3EUjR9FUM8e36A5auC1`</summary>

```text
99	
100	\paragraph{Additional directions.} Ten further experiments confirm the pattern (Appendix Table~\ref{tab:additional_interventions}). Formal verification via code generation (PROVE) achieves only 15.2\% compilable programs with $\Delta = -1.9$\,pp. Iterative CEGIS refinement oscillates after round~1 without convergence. Logprob-based filtering (DeepConf) finds near-zero separation between correct and incorrect traces ($\Delta_{\text{logprob}} = 0.006$). Cross-model SICA with three families reduces $\kappa$ from 0.53 to 0.42 but yields $\Delta = -0.49$\,pp ($p = 1.0$). Problem perturbation \emph{increases} $\kappa$ to 0.70 (anti-effect). MIN-UNSAT objective produces $\Delta = -6.5$\,pp. $\kappa$-aware weighted voting yields $\Delta = 0$ across 11 conditions. Z3-based trace filtering achieves 49.1\% precision with $\Delta = -0.49$\,pp. Bootstrapped gold constraints oscillate like CEGIS. ASCII format perturbation yields negligible $\kappa$ reduction (0.39 $\to$ 0.36).
101	
102	\paragraph{Independence-accuracy trade-off.} Table~\ref{tab:kappa_generalization} compares Fleiss' $\kappa$ across four aggregation methods on Mistral-7B FOLIO and adds SC from three model families. SC ($\kappa = 0.44$), Diverse-Prompt ($\kappa = 0.40$), and ICG ($\kappa = 0.47$) cluster at $\kappa \approx 0.40$--$0.47$, forming a same-model ceiling. Debate breaks through to $\kappa = 0.15$ ($K_{\text{eff}} = 4.44$) but collapses accuracy by $-18.6$\,pp. All three model families cluster above $\kappa = 0.40$, confirming the ceiling is architecture-independent (Appendix~\ref{app:cross-model-kappa}). This constitutes a Pareto frontier (Figure~\ref{fig:kappa-accuracy}): methods that increase trace independence destroy accuracy, while methods that preserve accuracy cannot break the correlation floor. Cross-model SICA (Mistral+LLaMA+Qwen, $K{=}12$) provides direct evidence: mixing three families reduces $\kappa$ from 0.53 to 0.42 yet yields $\Delta = -0.49$\,pp ($p = 1.0$)---even cross-architecture diversity is insufficient within the self-extraction paradigm. The only demonstrated path past this frontier requires signal from outside the model's distribution, consistent with the oracle result (\S\ref{sec:oracle}) and published systems that bypass self-extraction \citep{xu2026dylos, li2025aristotle, wang2025fover, pan2023logiclm}.
103	
104	\begin{table}[t]
105	\centering
106	\small
```

The only demonstrated path past this frontier requires signal from outside the model's distribution, consistent with the oracle result (\\S\\ref{sec:oracle}) and published systems that bypass self-extraction \\citep{xu2026dylos, li2025aristotle, wang2025fover, pan2023logiclm}.\n\n\\begin{table}[t]\n\\centering\n\\small",

        "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_3/projects/symb_invariant_consensus/docs/paper/analysis.tex",
        "content": "\\section{Analysis}\n\\label{sec:analysis}\n\nThe regime-dependent pattern in \\S\\ref{sec:main-results} establishes \\emph{where} SICA succeeds and fails. This section investigates \\emph{why}: trace correlation quantifies the SC plateau (\\S\\ref{sec:effective-k}), symbolic confirmation bias identifies the root cause (\\S\\ref{sec:confirmation-bias}), process-level independence reveals a deeper bottleneck (\\S\\ref{sec:process-kappa}), supporting diagnostics confirm the mechanism (\\S\\ref{sec:diagnostics}), and more than twenty remediation strategies demonstrate a fundamental independence-accuracy trade-off (\\S\\ref{sec:remediation}).\n\n\\subsection{Condorcet Effective-K}\n\\label{sec:effective-k}\n\nTable~\\ref{tab:independence} reports Fleiss' $\\kappa$ and Effective-K (Eq.~\\ref{eq:effk}) across 15 conditions at $K = 12$. Among instruction-tuned models, $\\kappa$ ranges from 0.21 to 0.82, yielding $K_{\\text{eff}} = 1.20\\text{--}3.62$. Qwen3-14B in thinking mode reaches $\\kappa = 0.91$ ($K_{\\text{eff}} = 1.09$). The four Mistral-7B FOLIO seeds produce $\\kappa \\in [0.41, 0.51]$ ($K_{\\text{eff}} \\approx 1.8\\text{--}2.2$), yet $\\Delta$ ranges from $+3.43$ to $-0.49$\\,pp---seed-level constraint quality, not independence, drives the variation. Two conditions are jointly necessary for positive $\\Delta$: sufficient $K_{\\text{eff}}$ (Qwen conditions with $K_{\\text{eff}} \\leq 1.30$ never improve) and sufficient SC accuracy (Mistral on ProofWriter with the highest $K_{\\text{eff}} = 3.62$ but SC $= 39\\%$ produces significant degradation). Even when both hold, sampling variance determines whether the constraint pool is discriminative. Cross-model validation with three families confirms universality: Mistral-7B ($\\kappa = 0.44$), LLaMA-3.1-8B ($\\kappa = 0.41$), Qwen2.5-7B ($\\kappa = 0.66$), and Qwen2.5-14B ($\\kappa = 0.74$) all produce $\\kappa$ well above zero (Table~\\ref{tab:kappa_generalization}; Appendix~\\ref{app:cross-model-kappa}).\n\n\\begin{table}[t]\n\\centering\n\\small\n\\caption{Trace independence metrics ($K = 12$, sorted by $\\kappa$ descending). $\\kappa$: Fleiss' Kappa. $K_{\\text{eff}}$: Effective-K (Eq.~\\ref{eq:effk}). SC: Self-Consistency baseline. $\\Delta$: SICA $-$ SC (pp). {*}\\,$p < 0.05$; positive $\\Delta$ values do not reach significance. multi-T $= T \\in \\{0.3, 0.7, 1.2\\}$, 4 traces each.}\n\\label{tab:independence}\n\\begin{tabular}{@{}llccrr@{}}\n\\toprule\nModel & Condition & $\\kappa$ & $K_{\\text{eff}}$ & SC (\\%) & $\\Delta$ (pp) \\\\\n\\midrule\nQwen3 (think) & FOLIO & 0.91 & 1.09 & ${\\sim}$84 & ${\\sim}$0 \\\\\nQwen2.5-14B & LogiQA & 0.82 & 1.20 & 78.50 & $-$0.50 \\\\\nQwen3 & FOLIO & 0.77 & 1.26 & 84.80 & $-$0.49 \\\\\nQwen2.5-14B & FOLIO & 0.74 & 1.30 & 75.49 & $-$0.98 \\\\\nQwen2.5-7B & FOLIO & 0.66 & 1.46 & 75.98 & $+$0.49 \\\\\n\\addlinespace\nMistral-7B ($T{=}0.3$) & FOLIO & 0.53 & 1.77 & 58.33 & $-$1.47 \\\\\nMistral-7B & LogiQA & 0.51 & 1.81 & 51.00 & $+$0.50 \\\\\nMistral-7B (s456) & FOLIO & 0.51 & 1.82 & 55.88 & 0.00 \\\\\nMistral-7B (s42) & FOLIO & 0.50 & 1.84 & 55.88 & $+$3.43 \\\\\nMistral-7B (s123) & FOLIO & 0.49 & 1.88 & 54.41 & $+$2.94 \\\\\nMistral-7B (s789) & FOLIO & 0.41 & 2.17 & 54.41 & $-$0.49 \\\\\nMistral-7B ($T{=}0.5$) & FOLIO & 0.52 & 1.80 & 58.33 & $-$0.98 \\\\\nMistral-7B (multi-T) & FOLIO & 0.48 & 1.91 & 57.84 & 0.00 \\\\\nMistral-7B ($T{=}1.0$) & FOLIO & 0.48 & 1.91 & 55.39 & 0.00 \\\\\nMistral-7B & PW-D5 & 0.21 & 3.62 & 39.33 & $\\mathbf{-2.83}${*} \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}\n\\end{table}\n\n\n\\subsection{Symbolic Confirmation Bias}\n\\label{sec:confirmation-bias}\n\nThe correlation above manifests through a specific mechanism: self-extracted constraints systematically confirm the model's majority answer regardless of correctness. On Mistral-7B FOLIO ($n = 204$), when SICA errs, 83.3\\% of extracted constraints support the incorrect majority answer---a bias ratio of $4.97\\times$ compared to correct problems. Consequently, 94\\% of SICA errors overlap with SC errors: symbolic aggregation adds no independent corrective signal.\n\nThe 12 instances where SICA corrects SC errors reveal the mechanism's boundary: 43\\% of these flips occur when a minority trace provides gold-aligned constraints, suggesting constraint diversity---not MAX-SAT---drives the rare successes. The causal chain is: the model generates an answer, then extracts FOL consistent with that answer; aggregation over such constraints reduces to a weighted majority vote (Eq.~\\ref{eq:degenerate}).\n\nIterative refinement does not break this cycle. CEGIS achieves $\\Delta = +3.43$\\,pp in round~1 but oscillates in rounds~2--5 without convergence, as each step reintroduces model bias. Cross-model extraction (Qwen3-14B or Qwen2.5-14B extracting from Mistral-7B traces) produces accuracy within $\\pm 1$\\,pp of self-extraction---the bias is a property of LLM formalization itself. The oracle ceiling (\\S\\ref{sec:oracle}) confirms that 75.9\\% of the potential gain is lost to self-extraction quality, localizing confirmation bias as the binding bottleneck.\n\n\n\\subsection{Process-Level Independence}\n\\label{sec:process-kappa}\n\nAnswer-level $\\kappa$ may underestimate the independence bottleneck: two traces can follow nearly identical reasoning yet reach different final answers, causing $\\kappa$ to treat them as disagreeing. On Mistral-7B FOLIO ($n = 204$), the mean pairwise cosine similarity of chain-of-thought traces (TF-IDF, Eq.~\\ref{eq:process-sim}) is $0.69$, far exceeding answer-level $\\kappa = 0.41$. These measures are uncorrelated ($r = -0.06$, $p = 0.36$): problems with diverse answers do not exhibit diverse reasoning. We find 9.3\\% of trace pairs converge on reasoning ($\\cos > 0.8$) while disagreeing on the final answer---apparent answer diversity masking process-level homogeneity.\n\nFigure~\\ref{fig:process-kappa} visualizes this decoupling: 204 problems form a flat cloud ($r = -0.06$), confirming that answer agreement carries no information about process-level similarity. This gap implies that interventions targeting answer diversity (temperature scaling, diverse prompting) may spread answers without altering reasoning paths. Process-level correlation establishes a tighter bound on effective ensemble diversity than answer-level $\\kappa$ alone.\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=\\columnwidth]{figures/fig3_process_kappa_scatter.pdf}\n\\caption{Per-problem answer agreement vs.\\ chain-of-thought cosine similarity (Mistral-7B, FOLIO, $n{=}204$). The flat regression line ($r = -0.06$, $p = 0.36$) shows answer-level and process-level independence are uncorrelated.}\n\\label{fig:process-kappa}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\\subsection{Supporting Diagnostics}\n\\label{sec:diagnostics}\n\nFour analyses confirm the mechanism (details in Appendices~\\ref{app:extraction}--\\ref{app:entropy}). Extraction taxonomy: 91.8\\% of constraints are discriminative, but their direction mirrors the majority; 42.9\\% are redundant across traces. Constraint ablation ($n = 804$): MAX-SAT and count-only agree on 94.4\\% of predictions---the solver adds no value over counting because constraint weights carry information redundant with vote counts. Hyperparameter sensitivity: $K = 12$ is the only setting with $\\Delta > 2$\\,pp among five values; $T = 0.7$ the only temperature with a positive effect---peaks coincide with low SC baselines. Entropy decomposition: all effects concentrate in high-entropy problems (up to $+12.8$\\,pp), but direction depends on accuracy regime.\n\n\n\\subsection{Remediation Analysis}\n\\label{sec:remediation}\n\\label{sec:interventions}\n\nWe test more than twenty remediation strategies spanning prompt engineering, method-level redesign, and ten additional directions. All produce neutral or negative results.\n\n\\paragraph{Prompt-level interventions.} Nine strategies spanning six dimensions (Appendix Table~\\ref{tab:remediation}) all fail. Contrastive prompting reduces non-discriminative constraints from 8.2\\% to 6.0\\%, but newly discriminative constraints still mirror the majority ($\\Delta = -1.1$\\,pp). Cross-model extraction fails in both directions: weaker models yield 0\\% discriminative constraints; stronger models produce accuracy within $\\pm 1$\\,pp of self-extraction. Multi-temperature sampling ($T \\in \\{0.3, 0.7, 1.2\\}$) yields $\\Delta = 0.00$\\,pp.\n\n\\paragraph{Method-level interventions.} Three strategies target different aspects of the independence bottleneck (Table~\\ref{tab:method_interventions}). Independent Constraint Generation (ICG) bypasses trace-constraint coupling by generating constraints directly from premises. Despite producing superior constraints---higher Z3 compilation, lower redundancy---ICG yields $\\Delta \\approx 0$ across three seeds ($p = .206$, n.s.). Per-constraint-set reranking produces $\\kappa = 0.47$, nearly identical to SC's $\\kappa = 0.44$: the bottleneck is a property of the model itself. Contrastive scoring yields $\\Delta = -9.31$\\,pp on FOLIO ($p < 0.01$); 62.7\\% of constraints are trivially satisfied. Debate produces $\\Delta = -18.63$ and $-23.00$\\,pp ($p < 0.001$ on both datasets), with discordant pairs heavily skewed toward correct$\\to$wrong flips. Three of six method-dataset conditions reach significance, all negative.\n\n\\begin{table}[t]\n\\centering\n\\small\n\\caption{Method-level interventions on Mistral-7B with McNemar's paired test. All produce neutral-to-catastrophic results. ICG shows two FOLIO seeds; $p$ for the first. {*}{*}{*}\\,$p < 0.001$; {*}{*}\\,$p < 0.01$.}\n\\label{tab:method_interventions}\n\\begin{tabular}{@{}ll rc rc@{}}\n\\toprule\n& & \\multicolumn{2}{c}{FOLIO ($n = 204$)} & \\multicolumn{2}{c}{LogiQA ($n = 200$)} \\\\\n\\cmidrule(lr){3-4} \\cmidrule(lr){5-6}\nMethod & Target & $\\Delta$ (pp) & $p$ & $\\Delta$ (pp) & $p$ \\\\\n\\midrule\nSC baseline & --- & 0.00 & --- & 0.00 & --- \\\\\nICG & Trace coupling & $-$1.96\\,/\\,$+$1.47 & .206 & $-$0.50 & .706 \\\\\nContrastive & Scoring bias & $\\mathbf{-9.31}$ & .007 & $-$4.50 & .160 \\\\\nDebate & Role diversity & $\\mathbf{-18.63}$ & ${<}.001$ & $\\mathbf{-23.00}$ & ${<}.001$ \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}\n\\end{table}\n\n\\paragraph{Additional directions.} Ten further experiments confirm the pattern (Appendix Table~\\ref{tab:additional_interventions}). Formal verification via code generation (PROVE) achieves only 15.2\\% compilable programs with $\\Delta = -1.9$\\,pp. Iterative CEGIS refinement oscillates after round~1 without convergence. Logprob-based filtering (DeepConf) finds near-zero separation between correct and incorrect traces ($\\Delta_{\\text{logprob}} = 0.006$). Cross-model SICA with three families reduces $\\kappa$ from 0.53 to 0.42 but yields $\\Delta = -0.49$\\,pp ($p = 1.0$). Problem perturbation \\emph{increases} $\\kappa$ to 0.70 (anti-effect). MIN-UNSAT objective produces $\\Delta = -6.5$\\,pp. $\\kappa$-aware weighted voting yields $\\Delta = 0$ across 11 conditions. Z3-based trace filtering achieves 49.1\\% precision with $\\Delta = -0.49$\\,pp. Bootstrapped gold constraints oscillate like CEGIS. ASCII format perturbation yields negligible $\\kappa$ reduction (0.39 $\\to$ 0.36).\n\n\\paragraph{Independence-accuracy trade-off.} Table~\\ref{tab:kappa_generalization} compares Fleiss' $\\kappa$ across four aggregation methods on Mistral-7B FOLIO and adds SC from three model families. SC ($\\kappa = 0.44$), Diverse-Prompt ($\\kappa = 0.40$), and ICG ($\\kappa = 0.47$) cluster at $\\kappa \\approx 0.40$--$0.47$, forming a same-model ceiling. Debate breaks through to $\\kappa = 0.15$ ($K_{\\text{eff}} = 4.44$) but collapses accuracy by $-18.6$\\,pp. All three model families cluster above $\\kappa = 0.40$, confirming the ceiling is architecture-independent (Appendix~\\ref{app:cross-model-kappa}). This constitutes a Pareto frontier (Figure~\\ref{fig:kappa-accuracy}): methods that increase trace independence destroy accuracy, while methods that preserve accuracy cannot break the correlation floor. Cross-model SICA (Mistral+LLaMA+Qwen, $K{=}12$) provides direct evidence: mixing three families reduces $\\kappa$ from 0.53 to 0.42 yet yields $\\Delta = -0.49$\\,pp ($p = 1.0$)---even cross-architecture diversity is insufficient within the self-extraction paradigm. The only demonstrated path past this frontier requires signal from outside the model's distribution, consistent with the oracle result (\\S\\ref{sec:oracle}) and published systems that bypass self-extraction \\citep{xu2026dylos, li2025aristotle, wang2025fover, pan2023logiclm}.\n\n\\begin{table}[t]\n\\centering\n\\small\n\\caption{Independence-accuracy trade-off (FOLIO, $n = 204$). Top: aggregation methods on Mistral-7B; bottom: SC across three model families. All families produce $\\kappa \\geq 0.41$, confirming an architecture-independent ceiling. $\\kappa$: Fleiss' kappa on raw answers.}\n\\label{tab:kappa_generalization}\n\\setlength{\\tabcolsep}{3.5pt}\n\\begin{tabular}{@{}llccrr@{}}\n\\toprule\nModel & Aggregation & $\\kappa$ & Eff-$K$ & $K$ & Acc.\\ (\\%) \\\\\n\\midrule\nMistral-7B & Debate & 0.15 & 4.44 & 12 & 38.24 \\\\\n& Diverse-Prompt & 0.40 & 2.21 & 12 & 54.41 \\\\\n& Self-Consistency & 0.44 & 2.02 & 10 & 56.86 \\\\\n& ICG & 0.47 & 1.93 & 10 & 51.96 \\\\\n\\addlinespace\nLLaMA-3.1-8B & Self-Consistency & 0.41 & 2.17 & 12 & 66.18 \\\\\nQwen2.5-7B & Self-Consistency & 0.66 & 1.46 & 12 & 75.98 \\\\\nQwen2.5-14B & Self-Consistency & 0.74 & 1.32 & 12 & 75.49 \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}\n\\end{table}",

        "content": "\\section{Conclusion}\\label{sec:conclusion}\n\nWe evaluated SICA across six models (three families), three reasoning domains, and over 3,000 instances with four-seed replication. The root cause of self-extraction failure is symbolic confirmation bias: on incorrect problems, 83.3\\% of constraints support the wrong majority answer (bias ratio $4.97\\times$), and 94\\% of errors overlap with SC. Process-level analysis reveals a deeper bottleneck: chain-of-thought cosine similarity ($0.69$) far exceeds answer-level $\\kappa$ ($0.41$), indicating reasoning homogeneity beyond what answer distributions suggest. More than twenty remediation strategies all fail to escape the resulting independence-accuracy Pareto frontier ($\\kappa \\geq 0.40$ across three families). Oracle controls confirm the mechanism is sound ($+14.22$\\,pp FOLIO, $+11.50$\\,pp ProofWriter); self-extraction captures only 24\\% of this ceiling.         "content": "\\section{Discussion}\n\\label{sec:discussion}\n\n\\paragraph{Symbolic confirmation bias as unifying root cause.}\nThe oracle experiments causally identify the failure source: gold constraints yield $+14.22$\\,pp on FOLIO and $+11.50$\\,pp on ProofWriter ($p < 0.001$) while self-extraction captures only 24\\% of this ceiling (\\S\\ref{sec:oracle}). Symbolic confirmation bias (\\S\\ref{sec:confirmation-bias}) unifies the two channels through which trace correlation degrades SICA. The \\emph{direct} channel limits voting diversity (Eff-$K < 2$ for many conditions); the \\emph{indirect} channel operates through constraint quality, as correlated traces produce homogeneous constraints that cause MAX-SAT to degenerate to vote counting (Appendix~\\ref{app:ablation}). Both stem from a single mechanism: the model generates an answer, then extracts FOL consistent with that answer. More than twenty remediation strategies fail because each addresses a symptom---diversity, filtering, alternative solvers---rather than this root cause.\n\n\\paragraph{Process-level implications.}\nThe gap between process-level similarity ($0.69$) and answer-level $\\kappa$ ($0.41$) implies that the independence bottleneck is deeper than answer-level metrics suggest (\\S\\ref{sec:process-kappa}). Interventions that successfully spread answers across candidates may still fail to diversify the underlying reasoning, explaining why temperature scaling and diverse prompting produce no SICA improvement despite affecting answer distributions.\n\n\\paragraph{Practitioner diagnostics.}\n$C_0$ combined with SC accuracy predicts when symbolic augmentation is unlikely to help: $C_0 < 5\\%$ implies degeneration; SC $\\geq 60\\%$ implies confirmation bias; SC $\\leq 40\\%$ implies error amplification. The $\\kappa$-based Eff-$K$ metric quantifies SC's own scaling ceiling: at Eff-$K \\approx 2$ (typical at $K = 12$), additional traces add negligible independent information.\n\n\\paragraph{Scope and paths forward.}\nWithin the Qwen family, $\\kappa$ increases from 0.66 (7B) to 0.74 (14B); whether 70B+ models or trained extractors shift the Pareto frontier is open. Published systems that report positive results \\citep{xu2026dylos, li2025aristotle, wang2025fover, pan2023logiclm} each bypass same-model self-extraction, consistent with our diagnosis.",

workflow\n  - research\n  - writing\n  - academic\n  - review\nversion: 1\nroles:\n  - paper_agent\n---\n\n# Step 5 — Iterative Refinement\n\nStep 4 全部章节落盘后，Paper Agent 进入 Step 5：把全文递交 Reviewer 评分，按反馈修改，直到收敛。\n\n**核心约束**：\n- 串行——一轮 Reviewer 返回结果处理完才进下一轮，禁止并发请求\n- 评分驱动——overall 提升才 accept 本轮，下降则 git revert\n- 上限 5 轮——硬约束，达到上限即停，不论分数\n\n## 状态记录（git 是 truth source）\n\n每轮修改前 commit 当前论文目录，作为 revert 锚点。无需额外 state 文件。\n\n```\ngit -C projects/{project} add paper/\ngit -C projects/{project} commit -m \"[paper] refinement round {N-1}\"\n```\n\nRound N 的 commit message 用 `N-1`，即 round 1 修改前 commit `round 0`（Step 4 落盘后的初稿）。\n\n## 单轮流程\n\n```\nRound N（N = 1..5）：\n\n1. git commit 当前版本（message: \"[paper] refinement round N-1\"）\n   └── 落地评分下降时的 revert 锚点\n\n2. 组装审查请求：\n   - read_project_file(\"paper_checklist.yaml\") — 用户自定义必查项\n   - 拼 LaTeX 全文（或 Patch 模式下的差异）+ writing_plan.yaml 路径\n   - 末尾追加 \"## 用户自定义必查项\"（见下方\"checklist 注入格式\"）\n\n3. paper_agent_request_review(text=组装后的请求, project=...)\n   └── 请求末尾追加维度提醒：\"除标准的 Originality/Clarity/Soundness/Significance/Presentation 维度外，请额外检查：叙事一致性（各章节是否讲同一个故事）、贡献-范围对齐（Intro contribution ↔ 正文 section 1:1）、去 AI 风格（em-dash、-ing、filler words）。参考 reviewer-audit-checklist §7。\"\n   └── Reviewer 通过 reviewer_post_to_director 回传评分\n\n4. 解析 Reviewer 响应：\n   - 维度分（Originality/Clarity/Soundness/Significance/Presentation，1-4）\n   - overall（1-10）\n   - 各条具体问题 severity（critical/warning/info）\n   - checklist 项 PASS/FAIL\n   ★ 任一 checklist FAIL → 该条问题 severity 自动按 critical 对待\n\n5. 按问题类型分流处理：\n   - 事实性错误（数字不一致 / 引用错位）→ 自己用 edit_paper_section 改\n   - 段落级叙事问题（逻辑断裂 / 强度词超限）→ spawn Worker 重写段落，回来再 edit_paper_section\n   - 结构问题（章节顺序 / 段落归并）→ 自己用 edit_paper_section\n   - critical 且 claim 本身无支撑 → 汇报 Director（不要自行降 claim）\n\n6. 全部问题处理完后判断本轮 overall 走势：\n   - overall(N) ≥ overall(N-1) → 保留本轮修改，进入收敛判断\n   - overall(N) <  overall(N-1) → git checkout 上一轮 commit，回退本轮所有修改，标记\"本轮无进展\"\n\n7. 收敛判断（任一满足即停）：\n   - 全部问题为 info（无 critical / warning）\n   - N == 5（硬上限）\n   - 连续两轮 overall 持平（差 ≤0.1）\n   - 连续两轮无新增 critical（critical 列表内容相同或更短）\n\n   未收敛且 N<5 → 进入 Round N+1\n   已收敛 → 进入收尾（见下文）\n```\n\n## checklist 注入格式\n\n`read_project_file(\"paper_checklist.yaml\")` 拿到 items 后，在审查请求末尾追加：\n\n```\n## 用户自定义必查项\n以下是用户指定的必须检查的项，每项必须明确标注 PASS 或 FAIL：\n- [chk_001] {text}\n- [chk_002] {text}\n...\n\n如果任何一项标注为 FAIL，整体 severity 自动提升为 critical（必须打回修改）。\n```\n\n- checklist 为空 / 文件不存在 → 不追加该段，正常请求\n- 不要重写 checklist 文本——保持用户原话，让 Reviewer 自己定位\n- Dream 模式禁止注入（Dream 论文不投，格式检查无意义）\n\n## 修改 Worker 任务模板\n\n段落级修改派 Worker（不是整章重写）时：\n\n```\nspawn_worker(\n  task_type=\"paper_writing\",\n  project=...,\n  task_prompt=\"\"\"\nReviewer 在 {section} 章发现以下问题，需要你修改对应段落：\n\n【问题位置】{paragraph_id / 行号}\n【问题描述】{Reviewer 原文}\n【建议方向】{Reviewer 给的方向}\n\n当前段落原文：\n```latex\n{原文片段}\n```\n\n参考资料：\n- 写作规范：Read skills/global/latex-paper.skill.md + red-section.skill.md\n- registry 切片：{相关 exp_id 列表}\n- 同章节已有内容：{section}.tex 整章\n- 引用：references.bib（只用已 verified 条目，未验证标 [UNVERIFIED]）\n\n要求：\n- 只改问题段落，不动其他内容\n- 输出修改后的段落 latex（标记 old / new）\n- 不要画图、不要补实验\n- 改完即返回，不写额外说明\n\"\"\"\n)\n```\n\nWorker 返回后 Paper Agent 用 `edit_paper_section(project, section, old_text=..., new_text=..., claims=..., table_cells=..., figures=...)` 落盘——`edit_paper_section` 自动同步 provenance，不会 deprecate 未触及的 claim。\n\n## 收敛后收尾（Step 5e）\n\nReviewer 评分循环收敛后**必须执行完整三件套**——视觉 + 引用 + 溯源**全部跑完**再汇报 Director；不允许只跑一两项就提交。任一发现 critical/blocker 都要在汇报中显式列出，不得\"建议提交审稿\"压过失败信号。\n\n### 三件套执行顺序\n\n按下面顺序串行（每一步完成才进下一步，前一步失败不绕过）：\n\n```\n1. paper-visual-review 排版检查\n   └── Read skills/global/paper-visual-review.skill.md\n   └── pdf_to_images(pdf_path=projects/{project}/paper/main.pdf, pages=\"1-N\", dpi=200)\n       逐页检查图表分辨率 / 字号 / 边距溢出 / 公式 / 引用编号 / 缺失引用占位 [?]\n   └── cleanup_pdf_images(output_dir=<上一步返回的 output_dir>)  # 不留临时文件\n   └── 收集 findings：[{page, severity, issue}]\n   └── 出现 critical（图表溢出 / 缺失编号 / 公式乱码）→ 记入 visual_report，**不**立即返工，\n       与 verify_references / check_provenance_completeness 的结果**一并汇报 Director**\n\n2. verify_references 最终引用验证\n   └── verify_references(paper_dir=\"projects/{project}/paper\", project=..., skip_online=False)\n   └── 返回字段：status (ok/orphan_bib/missing_bib/not_found/mismatch) 按条聚合\n   └── critical 阈值：\n        - missing_bib（正文 \\cite 但 .bib 缺）→ 必须修，否则 LaTeX 编译失败\n        - not_found（OpenAlex 找不到）→ 疑似幻觉引用，自己 WebSearch 二次验证；仍找不到 → critical\n        - mismatch（年份/作者偏差）→ warning\n   └── 自己能改的小问题（missing_bib、年份） → edit_paper_section 修；改完**再调一次 verify_references** 确认通过\n\n3. check_provenance_completeness 溯源覆盖\n   └── check_provenance_completeness(project=...)\n   └── 返回：tier_distribution / untracked_numbers / invalidated_claims\n   └── 关键阈值：\n        - Tier 3 占比 > 30% → 必须汇报 Director（红线，不得静默交付）\n        - Tier X（invalidated，实验失败/被作废）≥ 1 条 → critical：claim 失去支撑，汇报 Director 决策是否改 claim\n        - untracked_numbers（正文出现但 provenance 没追踪的数字）≥ 5 → warning：派 Worker 补 provenance\n```\n\n### 汇报 Director 模板\n\n三件套全部跑完后**统一**汇报（不要分三次发），把所有 critical/warning 列在一处便于 Director 决策：\n\n```\npaper_agent_post_to_director(\n  text=\"[写作进展] Step 5 收尾完成\n         迭代轮数：{N}/5\n         最终 overall：{score}\n         checklist：全部 PASS / 还有 X 条 FAIL（已修复 / 待 Director 决策）\n\n         ===== 视觉检查 =====\n         检查页数：{n}/{total}\n         结论：PASS / 发现 X 条 critical + Y 条 warning\n         {若有 critical：逐条列出 page + 描述}\n\n         ===== 引用验证 =====\n         总数 X 条，已验证 Y 条\n         critical：missing_bib X 条 + 疑似幻觉 Y 条（已尝试 WebSearch 二次验证 → 结果）\n         warning：mismatch X 条\n         {若有 critical：列出条目 + 已采取的修复}\n\n         ===== Provenance 覆盖 =====\n         Tier 分布：T1 {a}% / T2 {b}% / T3 {c}% / TX {d}%\n         {若 T3 > 30% 或 TX ≥ 1：列出受影响的 claim/figure/table}\n         未追踪数字：X 个（详见 untracked_numbers）\n\n         ===== 建议 =====\n         {以下任选其一}\n         A. 视觉/引用/provenance 全部 PASS → 建议提交审稿（submit_paper_to_review）\n         B. 存在 critical → 请 Director 决策：{具体问题列表 + 建议修复方向}\n         C. provenance 红线触发 → 请 Director 决定：补实验 / 改 claim / 接受现状\",\n  project=...\n)\n```\n\nDirector 决定是否调 `submit_paper_to_review`——Paper Agent **不**主动调，这是 Director 的权限。\n\n### 红线（Step 5e 不可绕过）\n\n1. **三件套必须全跑**：跳过任一项直接汇报 = 违规，被 Director 打回时不接受\"我以为不必要\"\n2. **critical 不静默**：视觉 critical / verify_references critical / Tier X ≥ 1 / Tier 3 > 30% 必须显式写进汇报\n3. **不主动提交审稿**：哪怕全 PASS 也只能\"建议提交\"，submit_paper_to_review 是 Director 权限\n4. **修复后必须复验**：自己修了 missing_bib → 重跑 verify_references；补 provenance → 重跑 check_provenance_completeness\n5. **Patch 模式走 Phase E**：Patch 的收尾在 Phase E（max 3 轮 Reviewer），不复用 Step 5e 全套；Phase E 自己做 check_provenance_completeness + verify_references，视觉检查可选\n\n## 硬约束（与 Paper Agent 红线一致）\n\n1. **修改前必 git commit**——没有 commit 不开新一轮，避免 revert 失败\n2. **未验证引用不进 .bib**——Reviewer 指出新引用需求时，先 verify_references 再写入\n3. **checklist FAIL 项必须修复**——不参与\"连续两轮无新增 critical\"的轮数计数，但下一轮必须解决\n4. **overall 下降必 revert**——不允许\"将就一下\"\n5. **不自行降 claim**——claim 无支撑是 Director 决策，Paper Agent 只能改表述强度，不能改 claim 内容\n6. **Patch / Dream 模式不走本 skill**——Patch 走 Phase E（max 3 轮），Dream 跳过 Reviewer 迭代\n\n## 与 Reviewer 评分模板的合约\n\nReviewer 在 `prompts/roles/reviewer.md` §5.3 已规定返回格式：\n\n```\n[写作评审] 轮次 N/5\n维度评分（1-4）：Originality / Clarity / Soundness / Significance / Presentation\nOverall（1-10）：\n## 用户自定义必查项（如审查请求含 paper_checklist）\n- [chk_id]: PASS / FAIL — 理由\n## 具体问题\n- [severity] 位置 + 描述 + 建议\n```\n\nPaper Agent 解析时按位置抓字段——Reviewer 偶尔会漏维度分，**漏了的当作 4（满分）处理**，避免误判 overall 下降。\n",
        "numLines": 241,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 241
      }
    },

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_015bSo4q6fiJKYXThSrzzULk`</summary>

```text
70	
71	\subsection{Remediation Analysis}
72	\label{sec:remediation}
73	\label{sec:interventions}
74	
75	We test more than twenty remediation strategies spanning prompt engineering, method-level redesign, and ten additional directions. All produce neutral or negative results.
76	
77	\paragraph{Prompt-level interventions.} Nine strategies spanning six dimensions (Appendix Table~\ref{tab:remediation}) all fail. Contrastive prompting reduces non-discriminative constraints from 8.2\% to 6.0\%, but newly discriminative constraints still mirror the majority ($\Delta = -1.1$\,pp). Cross-model extraction fails in both directions: weaker models yield 0\% discriminative constraints; stronger models produce accuracy within $\pm 1$\,pp of self-extraction. Multi-temperature sampling ($T \in \{0.3, 0.7, 1.2\}$) yields $\Delta = 0.00$\,pp.
78	
79	\paragraph{Method-level interventions.} Three strategies target different aspects of the independence bottleneck (Table~\ref{tab:method_interventions}). Independent Constraint Generation (ICG) bypasses trace-constraint coupling by generating constraints directly from premises. Despite producing superior constraints---higher Z3 compilation, lower redundancy---ICG yields $\Delta \approx 0$ across three seeds ($p = .206$, n.s.). Per-constraint-set reranking produces $\kappa = 0.47$, nearly identical to SC's $\kappa = 0.44$: the bottleneck is a property of the model itself. Contrastive scoring yields $\Delta = -9.31$\,pp on FOLIO ($p < 0.01$); 62.7\% of constraints are trivially satisfied. Debate produces $\Delta = -18.63$ and $-23.00$\,pp ($p < 0.001$ on both datasets), with discordant pairs heavily skewed toward correct$\to$wrong flips. Three of six method-dataset conditions reach significance, all negative.
80	
81	\begin{table}[t]
82	\centering
83	\small
84	\caption{Method-level interventions on Mistral-7B with McNemar's paired test. All produce neutral-to-catastrophic results. ICG shows two FOLIO seeds; $p$ for the first. {*}{*}{*}\,$p < 0.001$; {*}{*}\,$p < 0.01$.}
85	\label{tab:method_interventions}
86	\begin{tabular}{@{}ll rc rc@{}}
87	\toprule
88	& & \multicolumn{2}{c}{FOLIO ($n = 204$)} & \multicolumn{2}{c}{LogiQA ($n = 200$)} \\
89	\cmidrule(lr){3-4} \cmidrule(lr){5-6}
90	Method & Target & $\Delta$ (pp) & $p$ & $\Delta$ (pp) & $p$ \\
91	\midrule
92	SC baseline & --- & 0.00 & --- & 0.00 & --- \\
93	ICG & Trace coupling & $-$1.96\,/\,$+$1.47 & .206 & $-$0.50 & .706 \\
94	Contrastive & Scoring bias & $\mathbf{-9.31}$ & .007 & $-$4.50 & .160 \\
95	Debate & Role diversity & $\mathbf{-18.63}$ & ${<}.001$ & $\mathbf{-23.00}$ & ${<}.001$ \\
96	\bottomrule
97	\end{tabular}
98	\end{table}
99	
100	\paragraph{Additional directions.} Ten further experiments confirm the pattern (Appendix Table~\ref{tab:additional_interventions}). Formal verification via code generation (PROVE) achieves only 15.2\% compilable programs with $\Delta = -1.9$\,pp. Iterative CEGIS refinement oscillates after round~1 without convergence. Logprob-based filtering (DeepConf) finds near-zero separation between correct and incorrect traces ($\Delta_{\text{logprob}} = 0.006$). Cross-model SICA with three families reduces $\kappa$ from 0.53 to 0.42 but yields $\Delta = -0.49$\,pp ($p = 1.0$). Problem perturbation \emph{increases} $\kappa$ to 0.70 (anti-effect). MIN-UNSAT objective produces $\Delta = -6.5$\,pp. $\kappa$-aware weighted voting yields $\Delta = 0$ across 11 conditions. Z3-based trace filtering achieves 49.1\% precision with $\Delta = -0.49$\,pp. Bootstrapped gold constraints oscillate like CEGIS. ASCII format perturbation yields negligible $\kappa$ reduction (0.39 $\to$ 0.36).
101	
102	\paragraph{Independence-accuracy trade-off.} Table~\ref{tab:kappa_generalization} compares Fleiss' $\kappa$ across four aggregation methods on Mistral-7B FOLIO and adds SC from three model families. SC ($\kappa = 0.44$), Diverse-Prompt ($\kappa = 0.40$), and ICG ($\kappa = 0.47$) cluster at $\kappa \approx 0.40$--$0.47$, forming a same-model ceiling. Debate breaks through to $\kappa = 0.15$ ($K_{\text{eff}} = 4.44$) but collapses accuracy by $-18.6$\,pp. All three model families cluster above $\kappa = 0.40$, confirming the ceiling is architecture-independent (Appendix~\ref{app:cross-model-kappa}). This constitutes a Pareto frontier (Figure~\ref{fig:kappa-accuracy}): methods that increase trace independence destroy accuracy, while methods that preserve accuracy cannot break the correlation floor. Cross-model SICA (Mistral+LLaMA+Qwen, $K{=}12$) provides direct evidence: mixing three families reduces $\kappa$ from 0.53 to 0.42 yet yields $\Delta = -0.49$\,pp ($p = 1.0$)---even cross-architecture diversity is insufficient within the self-extraction paradigm. The only demonstrated path past this frontier requires signal from outside the model's distribution, consistent with the oracle result (\S\ref{sec:oracle}) and published systems that bypass self-extraction \citep{xu2026dylos, li2025aristotle, wang2025fover, pan2023logiclm}.
103	
104	\begin{table}[t]

          "-\\paragraph{Additional directions.} Ten further experiments confirm the pattern (Appendix Table~\\ref{tab:additional_interventions}). Formal verification via code generation (PROVE) achieves only 15.2\\% compilable programs with $\\Delta = -1.9$\\,pp. Iterative CEGIS refinement oscillates after round~1 without convergence. Logprob-based filtering (DeepConf) finds near-zero separation between correct and incorrect traces ($\\Delta_{\\text{logprob}} = 0.006$). Cross-model SICA with three families reduces $\\kappa$ from 0.53 to 0.42 but yields $\\Delta = -0.49$\\,pp ($p = 1.0$). Problem perturbation \\emph{increases} $\\kappa$ to 0.70 (anti-effect). MIN-UNSAT objective produces $\\Delta = -6.5$\\,pp. $\\kappa$-aware weighted voting yields $\\Delta = 0$ across 11 conditions. Formal verification via code generation (PROVE) achieves only 15.2\\% compilable programs with $\\Delta = -1.9$\\,pp. Iterative CEGIS refinement oscillates after round~1 without convergence. Logprob-based filtering (DeepConf) finds near-zero separation between correct and incorrect traces ($\\Delta_{\\text{logprob}} = 0.006$).
